## 13. The counting-convention rule, and the depositional limb

- ❗ **AN ISLAND COUNT IS A FACT ABOUT A DEFINITION.** ❗ State **who · when · what counts · what filter · what residual** — 🔑 **W-W-W-F-R**.
- 📰 **Official A&N:** **836 islands, islets and rocky outcrops**; **8,249 sq km** (Andaman **6,408**, Nicobar **1,841**); Nicobars *"some **22 main islands (10 inhabited)**"*.
- ✅ **Khullar:** **265** islands; about **203** Andaman; Nicobar **7 big + 12 small** plus *"several tiny"*; Andaman **6,596**, Nicobar **1,653**.
- ❌ **[N-06] 203 + 7 + 12 = 222 vs 265 → 43 unaccounted.** ❌ **[N-07] 265 is book-era**; official is **836**, a **threshold** difference, not a discovery.
- ❌ **[N-08] Both area splits total 8,249 EXACTLY; the partition differs by EXACTLY 188 sq km**, transferred from Nicobar to Andaman. ❗ **Agreed total, disagreed boundary.**
- ❌ **[N-26 / U-16] 38** inhabited in the Administration's **body text** and the NIC profile; **31** in the Administration's **own profile box**. ❗ **Both reported; neither chosen.** ⚠️ Same page: population **4,34,192 (Est.) 2019** against **3,79,944 (2011 Census)** — **different bases**.
- ❌ **[N-13] Nicobar count three ways:** Khullar **7 + 12** · Majid **18, 11 inhabited** · official *"some **22 main** (10 inhabited)"*. ❗ **The official figure governs.**
- 📰 **Lakshadweep official:** **12 atolls, 3 reefs, 5 submerged banks** = **10 + 17 + 4 + 5 = 36**; land **32 sq km**; **Bitra** smallest inhabited, **271 (2011)**; **220-440 km from Kochi**. ❌ **[N-09] Khullar: 25 islands over 108.78 sq km = 3.40 ×** the official area. ❌ **[N-10] Majid: 43 islands, 10 inhabited; *"about 109 km from Calicut"*.** ⚠️ **The *"10 inhabited"* agrees** — the books are **not uniformly wrong**.
- ❌ **[N-11] Bitra**, not *"Betra"*; **Amindivi**, not *"Amendivi"* — ❗ **Khullar's own Fig. 3.15 prints AMINDIVI**, contradicting his text.
- ❌ **[N-29 / U-06] No area is computed from *"50 × 25 km"*.** ⚠️ 1,250 against Khullar's own Nicobar total of 1,653 would leave **403 sq km** for all other Nicobar islands. ❗ Only ICRZ 2019's *">1000 sq.km"* is stated.
- ⚠️ **THE DEPOSITIONAL LIMB (Majid 2.42, "Offshore Islands"):** Ganga delta and Gulf of Mannar islands; **Piram · Bhaisala · Diu · Vaida · Nora · Pirtan · Karunbhar · Khadiabet · Aliabet · Butchers · Elephanta · Karanja · Cross · Bhatkal · Pegioncock · St. Mary · Anjidiv · Vypin · Pamban · Crocodile · Adunda · Sri Harikota · Pairkud · Short · Wheeler · New Moore · Ganga-Sagar · Sagar**; *"many ... uninhabited and **administered by the adjacent states**"*. ⚠️ Plus **Majuli** (fluvial) and the reef sites **Malvan** and **Netrani** (**04 DEC 2024**).
- ✅ **C2 §3.3:** dynamic deltaic islands near the Sundarbans — **New Moore / South Talpatti** — can be **created or removed**. ❗ **A delta island count is a count at a date.**
- ❗ **A complete Indian classification has FOUR limbs:** continental-festoon · volcanic · coral · **depositional**.
- 🔁 Endemism as biogeography → `Geography/basic/37` §12.6 and `Environment-and-Ecology/basic/05`. ❗ **Topic 11 does not cure the audit's open biogeography failure.**

## 14. The Arabian Sea system and the nested-area inversion

- 📰 **Composition:** **12 atolls, 3 reefs, 5 submerged banks**; **10 inhabited islands + 17 uninhabited islands and attached islets + 4 newly formed islets + 5 submerged reefs = 36**. ❗ **Derived, not quoted bare.**
- ❌ **[N-34] Scale:** **atoll** at archipelago and tract scale; ✅ Khullar's *"surrounded by **fringing reefs**"* at island scale. ❗ Both true.
- ✅ **The 11° N rule (Khullar 89):** north = **Amindivi**; south = **Cannanore**; *"in the extreme south is the **Minicoy** island."*
- ⚠️ **Channels:** **Nine Degree** (Minicoy ↔ main group; ❗ not the A&N divider) · ✅ **Eight Degree** (Minicoy ↔ **Maldives**; ❗ not within the main cluster).
- 📰 ❗ **NESTED-AREA HIERARCHY:** land **32** → lagoon about **4,200** (≈ **131 ×**) → territorial waters **20,000** (**625 ×**) → economic zone about **4 lakh sq km** (≈ **12,500 ×**). ❗ **Dispersion, not size, generates maritime extent.**
- ✅ **Asymmetry (Khullar 89):** *"Shallow lagoons ... on their **western side**"*; *"on the **eastern seaboard the slopes are steeper**."* ⚠️ Sediment accumulates on the **sheltered** side. ❓ **No cause asserted for the specific orientation.**
- 📰 **Settlement:** population **64,429 (2011)**; capital **Kavaratti**; **Bitra** smallest inhabited (**271**); **Bangaram uninhabited yet enumerated with 61 (2011)** — ❗ *"inhabited"* is **definitional**. 📰 Language Malayalam, *"**except Minicoy where people speak Mahl which is written in Divehi script** and is spoken in **Maldives** also"*. ⚠️ **Linguistic affinity follows sea distance, not administrative boundary.** ✅ **Coconut is the only major crop; fishing the main occupation; Minicoy has a light house and a weather observatory.**
- ❌ **[N-32]** The page prints *"**12º 13″**"* — **a seconds mark where minutes is meant**; read **12° 13′ N**. ❗ The same five features are called *"submerged banks"* and *"submerged reefs"* in consecutive sentences. ❗ Disclosed, not reproduced.
- 📰 **Stated once, then routed:** *"The entire indigenous population has been classified as **Scheduled Tribes**"* (**1956** modification orders); *"There are **no Scheduled Castes** in this Union Territory."* 🔁 `Social-Justice/basic/08`; `Polity/Scheduled-and-Tribal-Areas`. 🔁 UNCLOS, territorial sea and EEZ **as law** → `International-Relations/basic/04`.

## 15. ICRZ 2019 — the area gate and the two tiers

- 📰 ❗ **Instrument: ICRZ Notification, 2019, `S.O. 1242(E)`**, MoEFCC, **8 March 2019**, **GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY, PART II—Sec. 3—Sub-sec. (ii)**, No. 1106, Phalguna 17, 1940; under **section 3, Environment (Protection) Act, 1986**; report of the **Dr. Shailesh Nayak** committee.
- ❌ **[N-01] SUPERSESSION:** *"in supersession of **Island Protection Zone Notification 2011**, vide number **S.O.20(E), dated the 6th January, 2011**."* ❗ **IPZ 2011 is not current.** ❗ C2 line 70's *"CRZ clearances"* for Great Nicobar is corrected to **ICRZ 2019**.
- ❗ **Gazette discipline:** **CRZ 2019 = G.S.R., Sec. 3(i)** (mainland) · **ICRZ 2019 = S.O., Sec. 3(ii)** (islands).
- 📰 **Scope:** *"the coastal stretches of the **eight bigger oceanic islands** in Andaman and Nicobar namely, **Middle Andaman, North Andaman, South Andaman, Great Nicobar, Baratang, Havelock, Little Andaman, Car Nicobar** ... and the water area **up to territorial water limits**."*
- 📰 ❗ **THE AREA GATE:** **Group-I, *">1000 sq.km"***: South Andaman · Middle Andaman · North Andaman · **Great Nicobar** → **ICRZ HTL to 200 m**, **NDZ 100 m** (**50 m** eco-tourism). **Group-II, *">100 sq.km but < 1000 sq.km"***: Baratang · Little Andaman · Havelock · Car Nicobar → **ICRZ HTL to 100 m**, **NDZ 50 m** (**20 m** eco-tourism). ❗ **A statutory setback that is a function of island area.**
- 📰 **Provisos:** the A&N administration *"shall ensure that the concerns of the **fishing community** are fully protected"*; ❗ *"**The NDZ shall not be applicable in such areas falling within notified Port limits.**"*
- 📰 **Tidal influenced water bodies:** **HTL to 20 m or the creek width, whichever is less**; tidal limit at **salinity five parts per thousand** in the **driest period**; until ICRZ Plans are approved, **100 m or creek width, whichever is less**.
- 📰 ❗ **TWO TIERS:** *"**All the smaller Islands** in Andaman and Nicobar and **Lakshadweep**, other than those listed under the ICRZ categories, **shall also be covered**"* and *"shall be **managed through the respective Integrated Island Management Plans (IIMP)**"*. ❗ **NO LAKSHADWEEP ISLAND IS AMONG THE EIGHT.** ❗ *"Lakshadweep is subject to the ICRZ 200 m zone"* is **false**.
- 📰 **Categories:** **IA** — ❗ **corals and coral reefs**, mangroves with a **20 m buffer above 1,000 sq m**, dunes, biologically active mudflats, parks and **Biosphere Reserves**, salt marshes, **turtle nesting grounds**, **horse shoe crab's habitat**, sea grass, seaweed, bird nesting grounds, archaeological and heritage sites — mapped by **NCSCM** under Annexure-I · **IB** intertidal LTL-HTL · **II** urban, *"built up plots ... **more than 50 per cent**"* with drainage, roads, water and sewerage · **III** rural · **IVA** LTL to **twelve nautical miles** · **IVB** tidal water bodies bank to bank. 📰 Plans *"shall not normally be revised before **five years**"*.
- ❌ **[N-02]** PIB 2080596 (**04 DEC 2024**) says *"Corals and coral reefs are classified as **CRZ-IA** area under the Coastal Regulation Zone Notification 2011 and 2019"*; ❗ **for island reefs read ICRZ-IA**. ⚠️ MoEFCC replies of **13.12.2019** and **02.02.2026** also cite CRZ 2019 for the islands. ❗ **Disclosed, not pretended away.**
- ❌ **[N-31]** The gazette prints *"CRZ –I A"* and *"The CCRZ-IB"*. ❗ **Drafting defects; read ICRZ-IA and ICRZ-IB; strings not reproduced.**
- 📰 **HTL:** *"the line on the land upto which the highest water line reaches during the **spring tide**, as demarcated by **NCSCM, Chennai**."* ❗ A **demarcated** line. ❗ **Not the coastline-measurement line** — 🔁 `Geography/basic/10`.
- ❗ **[U-08] Amendment history NOT retrieved; no amendment number cited.** 🔁 Clearance procedure and appraisal machinery: **excluded entirely**.

## 16. Great Nicobar — the sums that close and the sums that do not

- 📰 **Locational (PIB, 1 May 2026):** about **40 nautical miles** from the **East-West international shipping route**; **over 20 m** natural depth at **Galathea Bay**; **ICTT 14.2 million TEU**; airport **4,000 Peak Hour Passengers** against *"approximately **1.8 million** passengers annually"* at Sri Vijaya Puram, opening at *"at least **1 million**"*, growing to *"approximately **10 million**"*; **450 MVA** gas and solar replacing diesel; stated purpose of reducing dependence on **Colombo, Singapore and Klang**. ✅ **147 km from Sumatra** (Khullar). 📰 **ICRZ Group-I** → **200 m / NDZ 100 m**, ❗ **NDZ inapplicable within notified Port limits**.
- ✅ ❗ **SUMS THAT CLOSE:** **72.12 + 45.27 + 48.71 = 166.10 sq km** · **35.35 revenue + 130.75 forest = 166.10** · **2.79 + 3.41 + 0.91 = 7.11 lakh trees** · **84.10 − 11.032 = 73.068**, and **76.98 − 73.068 = +3.912 sq km**.
- 📰 **7.11 of an estimated 18.65 lakh standing trees = 38.1 %.** 📰 **Phase I involves 40.01 sq km of tribal area.** 📰 **Tribal reserve 751.070 sq km**; **New Chingen**, **Rajiv Nagar**; **Shompen about 237**, **Nicobarese about 1,094**. 📰 **166.1 sq km = *"approximately 2 per cent of the total area of Andaman and Nicobar Islands"***; ⚠️ re-computed **2.014 %** against 8,249 — ❗ itself a **counting-convention claim**.
- ❌ **[N-36] 73.07 is the rounded form of 73.068**; the **+3.912** closes **only unrounded**. ❗ Disclose the rounding; never "fix" published figures.
- ❌ ❗ **SUMS THAT DO NOT CLOSE:** **[N-35]** **49.86 felling + 65.99 green zone = 115.85 vs 130.75 diverted → 14.90 sq km residual, unassigned**; and Phase-I diversion **48.65** beside felling area **49.86**, unreconciled. **[N-05]** backgrounder **97.30 sq km in Haryana** (ratio exactly **2.0** to 48.65) against FAQ **24,750.93 ha** with *"about **17,000 ha** in Haryana"* and **6,320.10 ha in Madhya Pradesh** → **23,320.10**, short by **1,430.83 ha**; **97.30 sq km = 9,730 ha**, not 17,000; **1,414.95 ha** called *"equivalent to the diverted area"* against **13,075 ha** — a factor of **9.24**. ❗ **Quote both, date both, do not average, repair or choose.** **[N-04]** *"A new township spanning **16,610 hectares**"* **= 166.10 sq km = the whole footprint**. ❗ **Cite 166.10 sq km; never repeat 16,610 ha as a township area.**
- ❌ ❗ **[N-03] THE DENOMINATOR TRAP:** backgrounder *"1.82% of **island forest cover**"*; FAQ, same day, *"1.82% of the **total forest area of A & N Islands**"*. ⚠️ Implied **130.75 ÷ 0.0182 ≈ 7,184 sq km**. ❗ **[U-03] The ISFR figure was NOT retrieved; ~7,184 is implied by division, never cited.** ⚠️ Against the island the diversion is **of the order of 12 per cent**. ❗ **Every use of 1.82 per cent names its denominator.**
- 📰 **Legal basis for off-island afforestation:** **MoEF&CC Guidelines dated 22.05.2019** under the **Forest (Conservation) Act, 1980**, exempting States and UTs with **over 75 per cent forest cover**.
- 📰 **Named once, then routed:** Prior EC under the **EIA Notification 2006** and **ICRZ 2019** with **42 conditions**; appraisers **ZSI, SACON, WII, IISc**, plus **IITs, NIOT, NCCR, NIO**; **three Monitoring Committees** plus an **Overarching Committee** chaired by the **Chief Secretary**; *"coral protection and translocation"*; **NOC from the Ministry of Tribal Affairs**; **Empowered Committee**; **NGT order of 3 April 2023**; *"**seismically sensitive and cyclone-prone**"*; **Great Nicobar Biosphere Reserve** and **leatherback turtle nesting** as named features.
- 🔁 **Four routes:** `Social-Justice/basic/08` and `Polity/Scheduled-and-Tribal-Areas` · `Environment-and-Ecology/basic/24`, `basic/11`, `basic/07` · `Geography/basic/33` and `Economy/basic/18` · `International-Relations/basic/04`.
- ❗ **[U-26 / R-16] A project factsheet is NOT a measured outcome.** ❗ Position stated **as of 1 May 2026**; no later milestone claimed.
